function O(e) {
    if (!e || typeof e != `object`)
        return !1;
    let t = e;
    return t.schemaVersion === 1 && typeof t.assetId == `string` && /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-5][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(t.assetId) && M(t.deliveryUrl) && M(t.thumbnailUrl) && [`image/png`, `image/jpeg`, `image/webp`].includes(t.mimeType || ``) && Number.isInteger(t.sizeBytes) && (t.sizeBytes || 0) > 0 && typeof t.checksum == `string` && /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(t.checksum) && Number.isInteger(t.dimensions?.width) && Number.isInteger(t.dimensions?.height) && (t.dimensions?.width || 0) > 0 && (t.dimensions?.height || 0) > 0 && re(t.association)
}

function re(e) {

        return !1;
    let t = e;
    return t.type === `collection` ? typeof t.id == `string` && typeof t.itemId == `string` : t.type === `idea_packet` && typeof t.id == `string` && (t.outputId === void 0 || typeof t.outputId == `string`)
}
function M(e) {
    if (typeof e != `string`)
        return !1;
    try {
        let t = new URL(e);
        return t.protocol === `https:` && !t.username && !t.password && !t.search && !t.hash
    } catch {
        return !1
    }
}

function At(e) {

                imageUrl: e.imageUrl,

            });

            e.media && t.push({
                media: e.media,
                resultId: e.resultId,
                imageUrl: e.imageUrl,
                collectionScope: jt(n, e.media)
            });
        for (let e of n.grids || [])
            for (let r of e.images || [])
                r.media && t.push({
                    media: r.media,

                    collectionScope: jt(n, r.media)
                })
    }
    return t
}
function jt(e, t) {

function En(e, t, n) {
    return t.split(/\n/).flatMap(t => {
        let r = t.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).flatMap(t => {
            if (e.measureText(t).width <= n)
                return [t];
            let r = []
              , i = t;
            for (; i; ) {
                let t = i.length;
                for (; t > 1 && e.measureText(i.slice(0, t)).width > n; )
                    --t;
                r.push(i.slice(0, t)),
                i = i.slice(t)
            }
            return r
        }
        );
        if (!r.length)
            return [``];
        let i = []
          , a = ``;
        return r.forEach(t => {
            let r = a ? `${a} ${t}` : t;
            e.measureText(r).width <= n || !a ? a = r : (i.push(a),
            a = t)
        }
        ),
        a && i.push(a),
        i
    }
    )
}
function Dn(e) {
    return e.split(/[\s,]+/).map(e => e.trim().replace(/^#+/, ``).replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}_-]/gu, ``)).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 8).map(e => `#${e.slice(0, 49)}`)
}

var kn = `vibe-atlas-saved-items`
  , An = {
    getSavedItems() {
        try {
            let e = localStorage.getItem(kn);
            return e ? JSON.parse(e) : {}
        } catch (e) {
            return console.error(`Failed to load saved items:`, e),
            {}
        }
    },
    saveItem(e) {
        try {
            let t = this.getSavedItems();
            t[e] = !0,
            localStorage.setItem(kn, JSON.stringify(t)),
            window.dispatchEvent(new StorageEvent(`storage`,{
                key: kn,
                newValue: JSON.stringify(t)
            }))
        } catch (e) {
            throw console.error(`Failed to save item:`, e),
            e
        }
    },
    removeItem(e) {
        try {
            let t = this.getSavedItems();
            delete t[e],
            localStorage.setItem(kn, JSON.stringify(t)),
            window.dispatchEvent(new StorageEvent(`storage`,{
                key: kn,
                newValue: JSON.stringify(t)
            }))
        } catch (e) {
            throw console.error(`Failed to remove item:`, e),
            e
        }
    },
    isItemSaved(e) {
        return this.getSavedItems()[e] === !0
    }
}

function ar(e, t, n, r, i, a, o) {
    e.save(),
    e.beginPath(),
    e.moveTo(n + o, r),
    e.arcTo(n + i, r, n + i, r + a, o),
    e.arcTo(n + i, r + a, n, r + a, o),
    e.arcTo(n, r + a, n, r, o),
    e.arcTo(n, r, n + i, r, o),
    e.closePath(),
    e.clip();
    let s = t.naturalWidth || t.width, c = t.naturalHeight || t.height, l = s / c, u = i / a, d, f, p, m;
    l > u ? (m = c,
    p = c * u,
    d = (s - p) / 2,
    f = 0) : (p = s,
    m = s / u,
    d = 0,
    f = (c - m) * .15),
    e.drawImage(t, d, f, p, m, n, r, i, a),
    e.restore()
}
function or(e, t, n, r, i, a, o) {

}

  , Lr = `vibe-atlas-plan`
  , Rr = 1
  , zr = `plan`;
function Br() {
    return new Promise( (e, t) => {
        let n = indexedDB.open(Lr, Rr);
        n.onupgradeneeded = () => {
            let e = n.result;
            e.objectStoreNames.contains(zr) || e.createObjectStore(zr, {
                keyPath: `imageUrl`
            })
        }
        ,
        n.onsuccess = () => e(n.result),
        n.onerror = () => t(n.error)
    }
    )
}
async function Vr() {
    let e = await Br();
    return new Promise( (t, n) => {
        let r = e.transaction(zr, `readonly`).objectStore(zr).getAll();
        r.onsuccess = () => {
            let e = r.result;
            e.sort( (e, t) => e.order - t.order),
            t(e)
        }
        ,
        r.onerror = () => n(r.error)
    }
    )
}
function Hr(e, t=qr(), n=new Date().toISOString()) {
    let r = e.displayResults?.length ? [{
        query: e.rankedBatches[0]?.query ?? `daily-grid`,
        results: e.displayResults
    }] : e.rankedBatches
      , i = []
      , a = new Set;
    for (let t of r) {
        for (let n of t.results)
            if (!(!n.thumbnail || a.has(n.thumbnail)) && (a.add(n.thumbnail),
            i.push({
                resultId: n.thumbnail,
                imageUrl: Gr(n.thumbnail),
                sourceUrl: n.link || `#`,
                title: n.title || e.vibeLabelEn,
                publisher: `${e.actorShortNameEn} · ${n.source}`,
                batchKey: t.query,
                gridPosition: i.length
            }),
            i.length === 9))
                break;
        if (i.length === 9)
            break

function Ei(e, t) {

        return 0;
    let n = 0;
    for (let r of e)
        t.has(r) && n++;

function Di(e) {

        return 0;

      , n = 0

function Oi(e) {

            familyLabel: e.batchKey || e.vibeEn || e.vibe
        }
    }
    )
}
function Fi(e) {
    return (e.match(/\p{Script=Han}/gu)?.length || 0) >= 2
}
function Ii(e, t, n=`standard`) {
    let r = n === `misprints`;
    return Pi(e.filter(e => Fi(e.actor) && !!e.legendaryMisprint === r), t.filter(e => r ? e.intent === `legendary-misprint` || !!e.legendaryMisprint : Fi(e.actor) && e.intent !== `legendary-misprint` && !e.legendaryMisprint))
}
function Li(e, t, n) {
    let r = new Map;
    for (let i of e) {
        let e = t(i);
        if (!e)
            continue;
        let a = r.get(e);
        a ? a.count += 1 : r.set(e, {
            value: e,
            label: n(i),
            count: 1
        })
    }
    return [...r.values()].sort( (e, t) => t.count - e.count)
}
function Ri(e) {
    return {
        actors: Li(e, e => e.actor, e => e.actor),
        vibes: Li(e, e => e.vibeEn || e.vibe, e => `${e.vibeEmoji} ${e.vibeEn || e.vibe}`),
        families: Li(e, e => e.familyId, e => e.familyLabel).filter(e => e.count >= 2)
    }
}
function zi(e, t) {
    return e.filter(e => (!t.actor || e.actor === t.actor) && (!t.vibe || (e.vibeEn || e.vibe) === t.vibe) && (!t.familyId || e.familyId === t.familyId))
}
var Bi = 3
  , Vi = 4;
function Hi(e) {
    let t = new Map;
    for (let n of e)
        t.set(n.familyId, (t.get(n.familyId) || 0) + 1);
    return [...e].sort( (e, n) => {
        let r = (t.get(n.familyId) || 0) - (t.get(e.familyId) || 0);
        return r === 0 ? (n.savedAt || n.capturedDate).localeCompare(e.savedAt || e.capturedDate) : r
    }
    )
}
function Ui(e, t) {
    let n = Hi(zi(e, t))
      , r = []
      , i = []
      , a = new Map
      , o = new Map;
    for (let e of n) {
        let t = a.get(e.familyId) || 0
          , n = e.publisher || ``
          , s = n && o.get(n) || 0;
        r.length < 9 && t < Bi && s < Vi ? (r.push(e),
        a.set(e.familyId, t + 1),
        n && o.set(n, s + 1)) : i.push(e)
    }
    for (; r.length < 9 && i.length > 0; )
        r.push(i.shift());
    return {
        slots: r,
        alternates: i,
        rationale: Ki(r, t)
    }
}

function Ki(e, t) {

        let e = n.get(t.familyId) || [];
        e.push(t),
        n.set(t.familyId, e)
    }

      , c = s.length ? s.join(` · `) : `whole saved collection`;
    return {
        aestheticRead: `${i.join(` / `)} across ${a.length === 1 ? `one vibe (${a[0]})` : `${a.length} vibes (${a.join(`, `)})`}, drawn from ${r.length === 1 ? `a single visual family` : `${r.length} visual families`}.`,
        whyTogether: r.length === 1 ? `All nine frames come from the same visual family (${r[0][0].familyLabel}) — the grid reads as one cohesive editorial issue.` : `The grid balances ${r.length} families — anchored by ${r[0][0].familyLabel} (${r[0].length} frames) with contrast from ${r.slice(1).map(e => e[0].familyLabel).join(`, `)} — so it reads curated, not scraped.`,
        motifs: o,
        suggestedStance: Wi.find( ({test: e}) => e({
            vibes: a,
            familyCount: r.length
        }))?.stance ?? `Aesthetic archive — a curated visual collection`,

        }
        ),
        manualSwaps: []
    }
}
function qi(e) {

`)
}
function Ji(e) {
    let t = 2166136261;
    for (let n = 0; n < e.length; n += 1)
        t ^= e.charCodeAt(n),

}
function Yi(e, t, n=new Date) {

            }

        }))
    }
}

var fa = [`Draft`, `In progress`, `Ready`, `Approved`, `Published`]
  , pa = class extends Error {

async function ha(e=fetch) {
    let t = await e(`/api/plan-posts`, {
        headers: ba()
    })
      , n = await Na(t);
    if (!t.ok)
        throw new pa(Pa(n, `error`) || `PLAN posts could not be loaded.`,t.status);
    let r = n && typeof n == `object` ? Reflect.get(n, `posts`) : void 0;
    if (!Array.isArray(r))
        throw new pa(`PLAN returned an invalid posts response.`,502);
    return r
}
async function ga(e, t=fetch) {
    let n = await t(`/api/plan-posts?id=${encodeURIComponent(e)}`, {
        headers: ba()
    })
      , r = await Na(n);
    if (!n.ok)
        throw Ma(n, r, `PLAN post could not be refreshed.`);
    let i = r && typeof r == `object` ? Reflect.get(r, `post`) : void 0;
    if (!i || typeof i != `object`)
        throw new pa(`PLAN returned an invalid post response.`,502);
    return i
}
async function _a(e, t, n=fetch) {
    let r = await n(`/api/plan-operator-scheduled`, {
        method: `POST`,
        headers: {
            ...ba(),
            "Content-Type": `application/json`,
            "Idempotency-Key": t
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
            notionPageId: e.id,
            expectedNotionVersion: e.version,
            expectedScheduledAt: e.scheduledDate
        })
    })
      , i = await Na(r);
    if (!r.ok)
        throw Ma(r, i, `Operator scheduling could not be recorded (HTTP ${r.status}).`);
    let a = i && typeof i == `object` ? Reflect.get(i, `execution`) : void 0;
    if (!ja(a, e))
        throw new pa(`PLAN returned an invalid operator scheduling response.`,502);
    let o = await ga(e.id, n);
    return o.execution.state === `reconciled` || o.execution.state === `operator_scheduled_receipt_pending` ? o : ka(o, a)
}
async function va(e, t, n=fetch) {
    let r = await n(`/api/plan-posts`, {
        method: `PATCH`,
        headers: {
            ...ba(),
            "Content-Type": `application/json`
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
            id: e.id,
            expectedVersion: e.version || void 0,
            ...t
        })
    })
      , i = await Na(r);
    if (!r.ok)
        throw Ma(r, i, `PLAN edit failed (HTTP ${r.status}).`);
    let a = i && typeof i == `object` ? Reflect.get(i, `post`) : void 0;
    if (!a || typeof a != `object`)
        throw new pa(`PLAN returned an invalid updated post.`,502);
    return a
}

function xa(e, t, n) {

        if (e.id !== t)
            return e;
        let r = n.status ?? e.status;
        return {
            ...e,
            ...Object.hasOwn(n, `scheduledDate`) ? {
                scheduledDate: n.scheduledDate ?? ``
            } : {},
            ...n.status ? {
                status: r
            } : {},
            ...r === `Published` ? {
                productionStage: `Published`
            } : {}
        }
    }
    )
}

function Ca(e) {
    return /^(rednote|both)$/i.test(e.platform) ? e.status === `Approved` ? Aa(e.scheduledDate) ? e.execution.state === `unavailable` ? {
        eligible: !1,
        reason: `Execution state is unavailable. Refresh before recording operator scheduling.`
    } : e.execution.state === `operator_scheduled_receipt_pending` ? {
        eligible: !1,
        reason: `Operator scheduling is already recorded.`
    } : e.execution.state === `reconciled` ? {
        eligible: !1,
        reason: `This post is already published and reconciled.`
    } : {
        eligible: !0,
        reason: ``
    } : {
        eligible: !1,
        reason: `ScheduledDate must be an exact time with a timezone.`
    } : {
        eligible: !1,
        reason: `Status must be Approved before operator scheduling.`
    } : {
        eligible: !1,
        reason: `Only Rednote posts use operator scheduling.`
    }
}
function wa(e) {

function ka(e, t) {

    return {
        ...e,
        execution: t,
        productionStage: n
    }
}
function Aa(e) {

function ja(e, t) {
    if (!e || typeof e != `object`)
        return !1;
    let n = Reflect.get(e, `state`)
      , r = Reflect.get(e, `scheduledAt`)
      , i = Reflect.get(e, `notionVersion`)

}

function Pa(e, t) {

var Fa = `America/New_York`
  , Ia = `Asia/Shanghai`
  , La = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;
function Ra(e) {
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(`en-US`,{
        timeZone: e,
        year: `numeric`,
        month: `2-digit`,
        day: `2-digit`,
        hour: `2-digit`,
        minute: `2-digit`,
        hourCycle: `h23`
    })
}
function za(e, t) {
    return Object.fromEntries(Ra(t).formatToParts(e).filter(e => e.type !== `literal`).map(e => [e.type, e.value]))
}
function Ba(e, t) {
    let n = za(e, t);
    return `${n.year}-${n.month}-${n.day}T${n.hour}:${n.minute}`
}
function Va(e) {
    if (!e)
        return {
            kind: `empty`
        };
    if (La.test(e))
        return {
            kind: `date-only`,
            date: e
        };
    let t = new Date(e);
    return Number.isNaN(t.getTime()) ? {
        kind: `invalid`,
        value: e
    } : {
        kind: `instant`,
        instant: t
    }
}
function Ha(e, t, n=Fa) {
    if (!La.test(e) || !/^(?:[01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/.test(t))
        return null;
    let r = `${e}T${t}`
      , i = Date.parse(`${r}:00Z`)
      , a = [];
    for (let e = -960; e <= 960; e += 30) {
        let t = new Date(i + e * 6e4);
        Ba(t, n) === r && a.push(t)
    }
    return a[0]?.toISOString() ?? null
}
function Ua(e, t, n) {
    let r = typeof e == `string` ? new Date(e) : e;
    return Number.isNaN(r.getTime()) ? `Invalid date` : new Intl.DateTimeFormat(`en-US`,{
        timeZone: t,
        ...n
    }).format(r)
}
function Wa(e) {
    return Ua(e, Fa, {
        hour: `numeric`,
        minute: `2-digit`
    })
}
function Ga(e) {
    return Ua(e, Ia, {
        weekday: `short`,
        hour: `numeric`,
        minute: `2-digit`
    })
}
function Ka(e) {
    return Ua(e, Ia, {
        weekday: `short`,
        month: `short`,
        day: `numeric`,
        hour: `numeric`,
        minute: `2-digit`,
        timeZoneName: `short`
    })
}
function qa(e) {
    if (typeof e == `string` && La.test(e))
        return e;
    let t = typeof e == `string` ? new Date(e) : e;
    if (Number.isNaN(t.getTime()))
        return ``;
    let n = za(t, Fa);
    return `${n.year}-${n.month}-${n.day}`
}
function Ja(e) {
    let t = typeof e == `string` ? new Date(e) : e;
    if (Number.isNaN(t.getTime()))
        return ``;
    let n = za(t, Fa);
    return `${n.hour}:${n.minute}`
}
function Ya(e, t) {
    if (!La.test(e))
        return ``;
    let n = new Date(`${e}T12:00:00Z`);
    return n.setUTCDate(n.getUTCDate() + t),
    n.toISOString().slice(0, 10)
}
function Xa(e=new Date) {
    return qa(e)
}
function Za(e, t=17, n=23) {
    let r = [];
    for (let i = t; i <= n; i += 1)
        for (let t of [0, 30]) {
            let n = `${String(i).padStart(2, `0`)}:${String(t).padStart(2, `0`)}`
              , a = Ha(e, n);
            a && r.push({
                instant: a,
                etDate: e,
                etTime: n
            })
        }
    return r
}
var Qa = Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries([{
    value: `A·Vibe`,
    label: `A · Vibe`
}, {

}, {

    label: `E · Dog`
}, {
    value: `F·Interview`,
    label: `F · Proverb`
}, {
    value: `G·Fan`,
    label: `G · Aerial`
}, {
    value: `H·Cdrama`,
    label: `H · Cdrama`
}].map( ({value: e, label: t}) => [e.charAt(0), t])));
function $a(e) {
    let t = e?.trim() ?? ``;
    if (!t)
        return ``;
    let n = t.match(/^([A-H])(?:\s*·|\s*$)/)?.[1];
    return n ? Qa[n] : t

function To(e, t) {
    if (!e || typeof e != `object`)
        throw Error(`CREATE handoff returned an invalid receipt.`);
    let n = Reflect.get(e, `receipt`);
    if (!n || typeof n != `object`)
        throw Error(`CREATE handoff returned an invalid receipt.`);
    let r = Eo(n, `createUrl`), i = Eo(n, `postId`), a;
    try {
        a = new URL(r)
    } catch {
        throw Error(`CREATE handoff returned an invalid Open in CREATE URL.`)
    }
    if (a.protocol !== `https:` || a.hostname !== `create.justlikekatie.com` || a.pathname !== `/compose` || a.searchParams.get(`postId`) !== i || Eo(n, `status`) !== `Draft` || Eo(n, `workflow`) !== `packet` || Reflect.get(n, `packetReceipt`)?.packetId !== t)
        throw Error(`CREATE handoff returned an invalid receipt.`);
    return n
}

async function Ao(e, t) {

    r.height = 1350;

    i.fillRect(0, 0, 1080, 1350),
    n?.memeRework) {

        i.fillText(n.title, 540, 120);

}
function jo(e, t, n) {

            return;

    }

function as(e) {
    let t = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(e)
      , n = t ? new Date(Number(t[1]),Number(t[2]) - 1,Number(t[3])) : new Date(e);
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(`en-US`,{
        month: `short`,
        day: `numeric`,
        year: `numeric`
    }).format(n)
}
